### Y2Player

## Install from a downloaded package

If this computer cannot reach the firmware archive, download the package on another device and hand it to Updater CE on the Install page. Updater CE finds the scatter file inside the archive, including in its `Firmware` or `SP_Flash_Tool` folder, and installs it without a network connection.[Get Updater CE](guide.html#download-updater)[Troubleshooting](troubleshooting.html)
